Rubino::Documents
  .to_markdown — gem-backed formats (when the optional gem is present)
    pdf -> extracted text, pages joined (pdf-reader)
    xlsx -> a per-sheet heading + GFM table (roo)
    docx -> heading levels, list items, and bold run emphasis (docx gem)
    scanned/image-only pdf -> a clear no-extractable-text note, not a crash
  format coverage / registry
    advertises at least the pure-ruby formats as available
    unknown / unsupported formats return nil so the caller falls back
  degradation — optional gem not loadable
    never raises out of to_markdown even on a converter exception
    returns nil for a format whose converter is unavailable, without raising
  .to_markdown — pure-ruby formats (always available)
    plain text with no known extension passes through verbatim
    plain code -> fenced with a language hint inferred from the extension
    csv -> a GFM table with header + separator row
    markdown passes through unchanged (not fenced)
    html -> headings, a list, an inline link, and a GFM table
    json -> pretty-printed inside a ```json fence
    xml -> pretty-printed inside a ```xml fence

    returns false (nothing presented) when no request is pending

API contract: files
  POST /v1/files (multipart)
    201 + descriptor when a 'file' part is present
    422 when content-type is not multipart
  GET /v1/files?path=
    422 when ?path is missing
    404 when the file does not exist
    200 + raw bytes with application/octet-stream
    422 on path traversal (Workspace::PathTraversal -> ValidationError)

  upsert updates the existing row for (provider, account_id)
  encrypts tokens at rest and decrypts on read
  lists connections, filters by provider, and destroys by id

    returns the child runner session's full transcript from the store
  approval-surfacing state
    flips an entry to :needs_approval and stores the gate + command
    lists entries awaiting approval
    counts a :needs_approval child as live (it still holds a slot)
    enforces MAX_CONCURRENT counting parked-on-approval children
    clears approval state and returns to :running on end_approval
  scoped-nesting caps (S1, single enforcement point)
    refuses past the depth cap (returns nil + :depth reason)
    clears last_refusal_reason after a successful reserve
    refuses past the global total cap (returns nil + :global reason)
    reads the caps from config (config-driven, not just the constants)
    refuses past the per-owner child cap (returns nil + :per_owner reason)
  live-activity fields
    appends finish lines to a bounded activity ring
    bumps tool_count and sets last_activity on record_tool_started
    starts a reserved entry with a zero tool_count and an empty log
    is safe under concurrent writes from multiple threads
    ignores activity for an unknown id (a late event after remove)
  tree helpers over owner_subagent_id (S1)
    ancestors_of walks owner_subagent_id up to the root, nearest first
    descendants_of returns the full transitive subtree (BFS)
    owned_by? is the direct-parent predicate
    children_of(nil) returns the human/top-level node's children
    children_of returns only direct children
  live output tail (#5)
    wipes the tail when the tool finishes (the output: block clears)
    caps a newline-free stream at OUTPUT_TAIL_LINE_MAX bytes
    keeps only the last OUTPUT_TAIL_MAX complete lines (+ the partial slot)
    stitches a partial line across chunks (a shell write has no line contract)
    splits chunks on newlines, keeping the partial line in the last slot
  stop request (#108/#13)
    flips a live entry to :stopping and reports true
    records a normal completion on a :stopping entry truthfully as :completed
    keeps a :stopping entry live (it still holds a slot while unwinding)
    returns false for an unknown or already-finished entry
    records a failure on a non-stopping entry as :failed (unchanged)
    records a failure on a :stopping entry as :stopped
  ownership link (S1)
    stamps a human-spawned child with nil owner and depth 0
    recomputes depth from the owner entry, ignoring a stale depth hint
    stamps an owner-spawned child with the owner id and owner.depth + 1

  falls back to application/octet-stream for unknown extensions

Rubino::Tools::FuzzyMatch
  .find_spans
    maps a length-changing NFKC ligature back to the correct original span
    returns an empty array when the normalized needle is absent
    locates a needle whose trailing whitespace the file added
    normalizes exotic (non-breaking) spaces to a regular space
    returns nil for a needle that normalizes to empty
    locates a smart-quote needle against ASCII quotes (original span)
    returns multiple spans for an ambiguous needle
    locates an em-dash needle against an ASCII hyphen
  .splice
    replaces each span with new bytes, back-to-front, preserving the rest

Rubino::Commands::Executor#busy_disposition
  classifies every registered built-in as :immediate or :blocked (no built-in falls through to :pass)
  :pass — not a recognized local built-in
    passes an @file mention line
    passes a `?` probe and a `!` shell escape
    passes an UNKNOWN slash command (so the post-turn dispatch handles it unchanged)
    passes plain free text
  :blocked — state-mutating / turn-affecting built-ins
    classifies /memory as :blocked (default-to-blocked on uncertainty)
    classifies /probe as :blocked (default-to-blocked on uncertainty)
    classifies /sessions as :blocked (default-to-blocked on uncertainty)

Rubino::Tools::ReadTracker
  records a read and returns its stashed mtime
  resolves symlinks: read via the link and read via the target are the same entry
  returns nil mtime for paths it never saw
  tolerates nil / empty paths without raising
  canonicalizes paths so a read via relative path matches an edit via absolute path
  is empty on construction
  #fresh?
    is STALE when content changed and the mtime went backwards (clock skew / restore)
    is fresh when the on-disk content is unchanged
    is STALE when content changed but the mtime is identical (coarse-mtime FS)
    is fresh after a no-op touch (mtime bumped, content identical)
  .for_session
    isolates sessions from each other
    hands a nil/empty session id a throwaway tracker
    still demands a re-read when the file content changed on disk between turns
    returns the same instance for the same session id across turns
    reset! clears the registry (test isolation)
    carries a turn-1 read into a turn-2 executor: the edit gate passes without a re-read

Rubino::Session::Store
  #last_id
    returns the newest message id (rowid tie-break)
    is nil for an empty session
  #delete_from_inclusive (undo/retry rewind)
    deletes the message and everything after it
    re-seeds the extraction cursor to the new tail after a delete
    does not seal an un-mined survivor between the old cursor and the cut
    clears the cursor when the delete empties the session
  #token_sum
    returns 0 when no messages
    returns sum of token_count
  #for_session
    returns messages in chronological order
    breaks created_at ties on rowid to preserve insertion order
  #since (memory-extraction cursor, #249)
    returns nothing when the cursor is already the newest message
    returns an out-of-order (backdated created_at) message newer than the cursor
    returns all messages in order when the cursor is nil
    splits same-second inserts on rowid (no overlap, no skip)
    returns only messages strictly newer than the cursor id
  #count
    returns the correct message count
  #seed_extraction_cursor (MEM-2)
    sets the cursor to nil for an empty session
    is a no-op when the session row is absent
    pins the watermark to the session's current last message
  #reseed_extraction_cursor_clamped (MEM-1 clamp)
    keeps a nil cursor nil (never-extracted session stays fully un-mined)
    falls back to the surviving tail when the old cursor was deleted
    is a no-op when the session row is absent
    leaves the cursor put when it still resolves (later survivors stay un-mined)
  #create
    creates and persists a message
  #recent
    returns the N most recent messages in order
    returns all messages when count exceeds total

Rubino::UI::API
  #confirm enriched approval.required payload

  #confirm decision -> persistence mapping
    session is in-memory only — no persistence
    the legacy 'always' alias persists the narrow rule (== always_command)
    deny_always persists a permissions:deny rule (prefix-scoped) and still returns false
    once and deny never persist
    always_prefix persists the derived prefix to command_allowlist
    always_command persists the narrow (exact command) rule
    plain deny is one-off — denies but persists NO deny rule (re-prompts next session)

    syncs the child's message_count to its real transcript size
    produces a child wire list with no orphan tool pairs
    preserves metadata[:tool_calls] and token_count into head and tail
    rolls back the summary and child on a crash mid-compaction (atomic)
    #thrashing? (anti-thrash back-off)
      follows the parent lineage chain across compaction children
      is false when a recent compaction was effective (>=10%)
      is true when the last two compactions each saved <10%
      is false with no prior compactions
      is false when the NEWEST same-second compaction was effective (tiebreak)
  #compact! below the token threshold
    no-ops with reason :below_threshold — no summary call, no child fork
  #compact!
    raises CompactionError when the session is missing
    a skipped result carries the minimum_messages threshold (#420)
    returns a skipped result when message count is below the minimum

Rubino::API::Operations::Runs::CreateOperation
  raises ValidationError when input is missing
  creates a run and dispatches it
  accepts attachments with no input key at all
  raises ValidationError when input is blank and there are no attachments
  accepts an image-only run: blank input with attachments
  raises NotFoundError when the session does not exist

  pipefail semantics (#156)

  flushes an unterminated leading <think> as thinking at end of stream

  recovers a leading tag split across chunk boundaries

API contract: runs
  POST /v1/sessions/:id/runs
    422 when :input is missing
    404 when the session does not exist
    201 + run payload when the session exists
  POST /v1/sessions/:id/retry
    404 when the session does not exist
    202 + run payload when a user message exists
    409 when the session has no user message
  GET /v1/runs/:id/events
    404 when the run does not exist
    sets SSE headers when the run exists and is already terminal
  POST /v1/runs/:id/stop
    200 + status=stop_requested and flips the DB flag
    404 when the run does not exist
  POST /v1/sessions/:id/undo
    200 + removed_messages count
    404 when the session does not exist
    409 when there is nothing to undo

    strips OSC window-title / clipboard sequences
    keeps tab and newline (legitimate layout)
    strips the CSI clear-screen + SGR color sequences, keeping the text
    renders stripped control bytes as visible caret notation (no silent delete)
    normalizes a bare CR to a newline so overwrite-spoofing can't rewind
    collapses CRLF to a single LF
    scrubs invalid UTF-8 before sanitizing (binary output is safe to print)
    neutralizes the single-byte C1 CSI introducer (U+009B) too
  .truncate spill seam
    calls the spill callback with the full pre-truncation text and references its path
    does not call the spill callback when within budget
  .truncate peak-memory bounds (#373)
    makes the under-cap line decision without splitting the buffer into .lines
    still produces a tail-biased, byte-capped result for a huge over-cap buffer
    decides over/under-cap without materializing .lines on the full buffer
  .scrub_utf8
    strips NUL bytes (valid UTF-8 but fatal to SQLite)
    scrubs binary bytes into a JSON-encodable, valid UTF-8 string
    produces a string that actually inserts into SQLite (no unrecognized token)
    passes valid multibyte UTF-8 through unchanged
    re-encodes BINARY-tagged output so JSON.generate doesn't see ASCII-8BIT
    returns valid ASCII/UTF-8 text untouched and identical
  .preview
    reports the exact number of omitted lines in the marker
    leaves single-line input alone
    honours custom head, tail, and max
    is a pure function — does not mutate the input
    returns empty string when input is nil or empty
    returns the full text unchanged when line count is at or below the cap
    trims to head + marker + tail when above the cap

Rubino::Agent::DegenerateResponseRecovery
  rung 2 — prior-turn content fallback
    reuses prior-turn content when all prior tools were housekeeping
    does NOT fire when prior tools were substantive (housekeeping flag false)
  rung 7 — terminal
    raises (returns :raise) once the prefill AND empty budgets are spent
  rung 3 — post-tool empty nudge
    does NOT nudge when no recent tool message exists
    fires at most once per turn
    appends an assistant + user nudge and asks to re-issue
    does NOT nudge a thinking-only response after a tool round — that routes to prefill
  rung 1 — partial-stream recovery
    uses already-streamed visible content as the final response
    does NOT fire when the streamed text is itself thinking-only
  rung 4 — thinking-only prefill-to-continue
    prefills (twice) for an inline <think> response then exhausts to retry
    prefills for a structured thinking field (no inline tag)
  rung 5 — empty-content retry
    retries a truly-empty response up to empty_max then raises
